{"step_index":4996,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-05-19T21:37:54Z","content":"The issue you described has been successfully diagnosed and resolved! \n\n---\n\n### 🔍 The Root Cause\nIn Firestore, all documents for the logging system are stored in a single flat collection called `traces`. This includes:\n1. **Parent Trace Documents**: Contain parent metadata (e.g. `trace_id` like `TR-1779225010034`, `user_input`, `completed_at`, `final_status`).\n2. **Child Step Documents**: Contain the granular reasoning fields (e.g. `traceId`, `step: 1`, `skill`, `observation`, `inference`, `decision`, `action`) generated dynamically by each agent.\n\nPreviously, the backend's `/api/traces` endpoint fetched *every document* inside the collection. This meant the mobile app was receiving both parent trace documents **and** individual child step documents mixed together:\n* The \"random numbers\" you saw on the cards were the individual child step documents being treated as top-level execution cards!\n<truncated 1527 bytes>"}
